\displaystyle A_{6}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s-\frac{2\sqrt{15}\sigma^{3}e^{(2a+1)^{2}\sigma^{2}}}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2K_{2}(1+a)\sigma^{4}\big(K_{2}^{2}(1+a)^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\big)e^{\frac{(a+1)^{2}\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}(a+1)\sigma)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{2\sqrt{15}\sigma^{3}e^{(2a+1)^{2}\sigma^{2}}}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-K_{2}(1+a)\sigma^{4}\big(K_{2}^{2}(1+a)^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\big)e^{\frac{2a+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\frac{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}(a+1)\sigma)}{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}a\sigma)}-1.

(F.32)

The first equality is true because for a normal random variable
z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
and any scalar
c
, we have
\mathbb{E}[z^{3}e^{-cz}]=\sigma^{3}(c^{3}\sigma^{3}+3c\sigma)e^{c^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}
, and
\mathbb{E}[e^{-c|z|}]=2e^{c^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-c\sigma)
, and we apply Fubini’s theorem to exchange the order of integration. The penultimate inequality is derived by applying Lemma
F.19
.
Following a similar calculation, we can also get the upper bound for
(I)
as

\displaystyle(I)\leq

\displaystyle A_{6}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s+\frac{2\sqrt{15}\sigma^{3}e^{(2a+1)^{2}\sigma^{2}}}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-K_{2}(1+a)\sigma^{4}\big(K_{2}^{2}(1+a)^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\big)e^{\frac{2a+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\frac{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}(a+1)\sigma)}{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}a\sigma)}+1.

(F.33)

To calculate the upper and lower bounds for the term
(II)
, we also first calculate the derivatives of
A_{1}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
w.r.t.
\lambda
as

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}A_{1}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\Big|-\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-(\alpha+a)(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}x_{1}^{2}e^{-\lambda e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|x_{1}|^{2}e^{-(\alpha+a)(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}\big]\leq 2\sqrt{3}\sigma^{2}e^{(a+\alpha)^{2}\sigma^{2}},

which implies that
A_{1}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
is Lipschitz continuous w.r.t.
\lambda
. Combined with the Lipschitz continuity of
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
established in the proof of Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle|A_{2}(\lambda,1,a,K_{1})A_{1}(\lambda,a,a,K_{1})-A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|A_{2}(\lambda,1,a,K_{1})-A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})|(|A_{1}(\lambda,a,a,K_{1})-A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})|+|A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})|)

\displaystyle+|A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})||A_{1}(\lambda,a,a,K_{1})-A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\lambda(a^{2}\sigma^{2}\!+\!1)e^{9(a\vee 1)^{2}/2}+12\lambda^{2}\sigma^{4}e^{9(a\vee 1)^{2}/2},

where the last inequality holds by using the Lipschitz continuous properties of
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
, and the facts that

A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})=-2\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma),A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})=2\sigma^{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(\!-\!a\sigma K_{1})
and
|K_{1}|,|K_{2}|\leq 1
. With the inequality established above and the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle A_{2}(\lambda,1,a,K_{1})A_{1}(\lambda,a,a,K_{1})\leq-4\sigma^{4}K_{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{\frac{a^{2}+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(\!-a\!K_{1}\sigma)+\lambda c_{2}(a,\sigma)+\lambda^{2}c_{3}(a,\sigma);

\displaystyle A_{2}(\lambda,1,a,K_{1})A_{2}(\lambda,a,a,K_{1})\geq-4\sigma^{4}K_{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{\frac{a^{2}+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(\!-a\!K_{1}\sigma)-\lambda c_{2}(a,\sigma)-\lambda^{2}c_{3}(a,\sigma).

(F.34)

Now, we are ready to derive the lower and upper bounds for the term
(II)
based on (
F.1
). We first derive the lower bound as

\displaystyle(II)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}A_{2}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})A_{1}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-4\sigma^{4}K_{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{\frac{a^{2}+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(\!-a\!K_{1}\sigma)\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s-\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-4\sigma^{4}K_{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{\frac{a^{2}+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(\!-a\!K_{1}\sigma)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\bigg]-\frac{2c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{3}}{\big(\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{3}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-2\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}K_{2}(a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}+1)\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)-\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n},

(F.35)

where
c_{4}(a,\sigma)
is a positive constant solely depending on
a
and
\sigma
. Here the last equation is derived by Fubini’s theorem to exchange the order of the integral, and the last inequality is established by Lemma
F.19
. Similarly, we can also obtain that

\displaystyle(II)\leq

\displaystyle-4\sigma^{4}K_{2}(a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)e^{\frac{a^{2}+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(\!-a\!K_{1}\sigma)\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s+\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-2\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}K_{2}(a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}+1)\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)+\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.36)

Lastly, by utilizing Taylor’s expansion regarding the function
\mathbb{E}[K_{2}(II)]=\mathbb{E}\big[-2\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}K_{2}^{2}(a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}+1)\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}\sigma)\big]
w.r.t.
K_{1}
at
K_{1}=0
, and the facts that
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}^{2}]=1/d
, we can derive that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big[K_{2}(II)\big]+\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}(a^{2}\sigma^{2}+1)\Big|\leq\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

(F.37)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.1
), (
F.1
), (
F.1
), and (
F.37
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.8
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}}\bigg]+\frac{2}{\pi}an\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Bigg|\leq f_{13}(a,\sigma)+\frac{nf_{14}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{13}(a,\sigma)
and
f_{14}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.8
.

Following a similar procedure in the proof of Lemma
F.3
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{1}}x_{1}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{1}}x_{1}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}A_{8}(s/n,1+a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}A_{7}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(II)}\bigg].

(F.38)

Here,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
,
A_{3}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{4}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
shares the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.3
. In addition, the terms
A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
are defined as:

\displaystyle A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})=\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\alpha K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-\alpha K_{2}x_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|x_{1}e^{-\lambda e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big];

\displaystyle A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})=\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}\alpha|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}\alpha x_{1}}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|^{2}e^{-\lambda e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big].

To calculate the upper and lower bounds for the terms
(I)
and
(II)
, we first calculate the derivatives of

A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
w.r.t.
\lambda
as

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\bigg|=

\displaystyle\Big|-\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-(\alpha+a)K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-(\alpha+a)K_{2}x_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|x_{1}e^{-\lambda e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|x_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{1}|e^{-(\alpha+a)K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-(\alpha+a)K_{2}x_{1}}|K_{1}\big]\leq 2\sigma^{2}e^{(a+\alpha)^{2}\sigma^{2}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\bigg|=

\displaystyle\Big|-\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-(\alpha+a)K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-(\alpha+a)K_{2}x_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|^{2}x_{1}e^{-\lambda e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|x_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{1}|^{2}e^{-(\alpha+a)K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-(\alpha+a)K_{2}x_{1}}|K_{1}\big]\leq 6\sigma^{2}e^{(a+\alpha)^{2}\sigma^{2}},

which implies that
A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
is Lipschitz continuous w.r.t.
\lambda
, and
|A_{7}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})-A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})|\leq 2\sigma^{2}e^{4a^{2}\sigma^{2}}s/n
,
|A_{8}(s/n,1+a,a,K_{1})-A_{8}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})|\leq 6\sigma^{2}e^{(2a+1)^{2}\sigma^{2}}s/n
.
Consequently, we can establish the upper and lower bounds for the term
(I)
as

\displaystyle(I)\geq

\displaystyle A_{8}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s-\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{8}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]-\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\big((1+(1+a)^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma)-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma\phi((1+a)K_{1}\sigma)\big)}{2\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}-1.

(F.39)

The first equality is true by applying Fubini’s theorem to exchange the order of integration. The penultimate inequality is derived by applying Lemma
F.19
, and the fact that
A_{8}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})=-2(1+a)\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{(1+a)^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\big[(1+(1+a)^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma)-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma\,\phi((1+a)K_{1}\sigma)\big]
.
Following a similar calculation, we can also get the upper bound for
(I)
as

\displaystyle(I)\leq

\displaystyle A_{8}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s+\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\big((1+(1+a)^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma)-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma\phi((1+a)K_{1}\sigma)\big)}{2\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}+1.

(F.40)

Combined with the Lipschitz continuity of
A_{4}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
established in the proof of Lemma
F.3
, we have

\displaystyle|A_{7}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})-A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})|\leq\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)s}{n}+\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)s^{2}}{n^{2}}.

Now, we are ready to derive the lower and upper bounds for the term
(II)
as

\displaystyle(II)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}A_{7}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s-\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\bigg]-\frac{2c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{3}}{\big(\sum_{i=3}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{3}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{2e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}-\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{n},

(F.41)

where
c_{5}(a,\sigma)
is a positive constant solely depending on
a
and
\sigma
. Here the last equation is derived by Fubini’s theorem to exchange the order of the integral, and the last inequality is established by Lemma
F.19
. Similarly, we can also obtain that

\displaystyle(II)\leq

\displaystyle A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s+\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{2e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}+\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.42)

To provide an exact lower and upper bound for
\mathbb{E}\big[\frac{K_{2}A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{2e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}\big]
, we consider its Taylor’s expansion at
K_{1}=0
, then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}A_{7}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{2e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg]+\frac{2}{\pi}a\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Bigg|\leq\frac{c_{6}(a,\sigma)}{d}

(F.43)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.1
), (
F.1
), (
F.1
), and (
F.43
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.9
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-(1+a)K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-(1+a)K_{2}x_{i}}x_{i}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}}\bigg]+(1+a)\sigma^{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Bigg|\leq\frac{f_{15}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{f_{16}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{15}(a,\sigma)
and
f_{16}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.9
.

Following a similar procedure in the proof of Lemma
F.5
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{i}}x_{i}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{i}}x_{i}\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{1}}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}x_{1}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+a)x_{1}}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}x_{1}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}A_{2}(s/n,1+a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg],

(F.44)

Here,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
.
By using the Lipschitz continuity of
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
established in the proof of Lemma
F.1
, we can establish the upper and lower bounds for the term
(I)
as

\displaystyle(I)\geq

\displaystyle A_{2}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\int_{0}^{\infty}[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s-\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}s[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{2}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]-\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}-\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.45)

The first equality is true by applying Fubini’s theorem to exchange the order of integration. The penultimate inequality is derived by applying Lemma
F.19
, and the fact that
A_{2}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})=-2(1+a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\frac{(1+a)^{2}\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma)
. Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle(I)\leq

\displaystyle A_{2}(0,1+a,a,K_{1})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n}{\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]+\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{n^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=2}^{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|+K_{2}x_{i})}\big)^{2}}\Big|K_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}+\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.46)

To provide an exact lower and upper bound for
\mathbb{E}\big[-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\big]
, we consider its Taylor’s expansion at
K_{1}=0
, then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[-\frac{(1+a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}e^{\sigma^{2}/2}\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\bigg]+(1+a)\sigma^{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Bigg|\leq\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{d}

(F.47)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.1
), and (
F.47
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.10
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}x_{i_{3}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]+na^{2}\sigma^{6}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f_{17}(a,\sigma)+\frac{nf_{18}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{17}(a,\sigma)
,
f_{18}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.10
.

Following a similar procedure in the proof of Lemma
F.2
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}x_{i_{3}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}x_{i_{3}}\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}x_{1}^{3}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+2a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+2ax_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+2n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)(n-2)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{3}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2}+ax_{3})}x_{1}x_{2}x_{3}

\displaystyle\cdot e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{3}|+K_{2}x_{3})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}x_{1}^{3}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+2a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+2ax_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{2(n-1)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{3}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2}+ax_{3})}x_{1}x_{2}x_{3}

\displaystyle\cdot e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{3}|+K_{2}x_{3})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{6}(s/n,1+2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})A_{1}(s/n,2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(II)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{2(n-1)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{1}(s/n,a+1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(III)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}^{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{2}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s}_{(IV)}\bigg].

(F.48)

Here,
A_{1}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
and
A_{6}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
shares the same definition as in the proof in Lemma
F.7
. Through similar procedures in the proof of Lemma
F.7
(by utilizing the Lipschitz continuities of these functions), we can derive that:

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{A_{6}(0,1+2a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+K_{2}(1+2a)\sigma^{4}\big(K_{2}^{2}(1+2a)^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\big)e^{\frac{2a^{2}+4a+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\frac{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}(2a+1)\sigma)}{\Phi(\!-\!K_{1}a\sigma)}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n};

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\frac{A_{1}(0,2a,a,K_{1})A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\sigma^{4}K_{2}\Big(1+4a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}\Big)e^{\frac{(2a^{2}+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\frac{\Phi(-K_{1}\sigma)\Phi(-2aK_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi^{2}(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n};

\displaystyle\bigg|(III)+\frac{A_{1}(0,a+1,a,K_{1})A_{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(III)+a\sigma^{4}K_{2}\Big(1+(a+1)^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}\Big)e^{\frac{2a+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\frac{\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.49)

Specifically, for the term
(IV)
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|(IV)+\frac{A_{2}(0,1,a,K_{1})A_{2}^{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|=\bigg|(IV)+a^{2}\sigma^{6}K_{2}^{3}e^{\sigma^{2}/2}\frac{\Phi(-K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.50)

Lastly, by utilizing Taylor’s expansion regarding the function
\mathbb{E}\big[a^{2}\sigma^{6}K_{2}^{4}e^{\sigma^{2}/2}\frac{\Phi(-K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\big]
w.r.t.
K_{1}
at
K_{1}=0
, and the facts that
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}^{2}]=1/d
, we can derive that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big[K_{2}(IV)\big]+a^{2}\sigma^{6}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Big|\leq\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

(F.51)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.50
), and (
F.51
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.11
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|x_{i_{3}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]+\frac{2}{\pi}na\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f_{19}(a,\sigma)+\frac{nf_{20}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{19}(a,\sigma)
,
f_{20}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.11
.

Following a similar procedure in the proof of Lemma
F.10
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|x_{i_{3}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{3}}|))-K_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}}+ax_{i_{3}})}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|x_{i_{3}}\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|^{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+2a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+2ax_{2})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)(n-2)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{3}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2}+ax_{3})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{3}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{3}|+K_{2}x_{3})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|^{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+2a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+2ax_{2})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(a|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+(a+1)|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}(ax_{1}+(a+1)x_{2})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)(n-2)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{3}|)-K_{2}(x_{1}+ax_{2}+ax_{3})}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|x_{3}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{3}|+K_{2}x_{3})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{8}(s/n,1+2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{4}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})A_{7}(s/n,2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(II)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{3}(s/n,a+1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(III)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{4}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{7}(s/n,a+1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(IV)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(s/n,1,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-3}\mathrm{d}s}_{(V)}\bigg].

(F.52)

Here,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
,
A_{3}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{4}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.3
, and
A_{7}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
A_{8}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
shares the same definition as in the proof in Lemma
F.8
.
Then, through similar procedures in the proof of Lemma
F.7
and
F.8
(by utilizing the Lipschitz continuities of these functions), we can derive that:

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{A_{8}(0,2a+1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{(1\!+\!2a)\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{\frac{2a^{2}+4a+1}{2}\sigma^{2}}\big[(1\!+\!(1\!+\!2a)^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(\!-\!(1\!+\!2a)K_{1}\sigma)\!-\!(1\!+\!2a)K_{1}\sigma\phi((1\!+\!2a)K_{1}\sigma)\big]}{2\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n};

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\frac{A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})A_{7}(0,2a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\frac{a\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\big(\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{\pi}}\sigma e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}}{2}}-2K_{1}\sigma^{2}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(-K_{1}\sigma)\big)\big(\sigma\phi(2aK_{1}\sigma)-2aK_{1}\sigma^{2}\Phi(-2aK_{1}\sigma)\big)}{\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n};

\displaystyle\bigg|(III)+\frac{A_{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{3}(0,a+1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(III)+\frac{a\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\Big(\sigma^{2}(1+(a+1)^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma)-(a+1)K_{1}\sigma^{3}\phi((a+1)K_{1}\sigma)\Big)\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{n};

\displaystyle\bigg|(IV)+\frac{A_{4}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{7}(0,a+1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|(IV)+\frac{(a\!+\!1)\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a\!+\!1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\big(\!\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{\pi}}\sigma e^{\frac{a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}}{2}}\!-\!2aK_{1}\sigma^{2}e^{\frac{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(\!-\!aK_{1}\sigma)\big)}{2\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\big(\sigma\phi((a\!+\!1)K_{1}\sigma)\!-\!(a\!+\!1)K_{1}\sigma^{2}\Phi(\!-\!(a\!+\!1)K_{1}\sigma)\big)\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{4}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.53)

Specifically, for the term
(V)
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|(V)+\frac{A_{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{4}(0,1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg|(V)+\frac{a\sigma^{2}K_{2}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\sigma e^{-\tfrac{a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{1}^{2}}{2}}-2aK_{1}\sigma^{2}\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)\Big)}{2\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}\Big(\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{\pi}}\sigma e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2}}{2}}-2K_{1}\sigma^{2}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(-K_{1}\sigma)\Big)\Bigg|\leq\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.54)

Lastly, by utilizing Taylor’s expansion regarding the function above w.r.t.
K_{1}
at
K_{1}=0
, and the facts that
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[K_{1}^{2}]=1/d
, we can derive that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big[K_{2}(V)\big]+\frac{2}{\pi}a\sigma^{4}e^{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Big|\leq\frac{c_{5}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{c_{6}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

(F.55)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.1
), and (
F.55
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.12
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}((1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}((1+a)x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]+a\sigma^{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\Bigg|\leq\frac{f_{21}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{f_{22}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{21}(a,\sigma)
and
f_{22}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.12
.

Following a similar procedure in the proof of Lemma
F.9
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}((1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}((1+a)x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}}{(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-K_{1}((1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-K_{2}((1+a)x_{i_{1}}+ax_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}x_{1}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}((1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}((1+a)x_{1}+ax_{2})}x_{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}(1+2a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-K_{2}(1+2a)x_{1}}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}x_{1}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-K_{1}((1+a)|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+a|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-K_{2}((1+a)x_{1}+ax_{2})}x_{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,1+2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{5}(s/n,1+a,a,K_{1})A_{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(II)}\bigg],

(F.56)

Here,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
, and
A_{5}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
shares the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.5
.
Then, through similar procedures in the proof of previous lemmas (by utilizing the Lipschitz continuities of these functions), we can derive that:

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{A_{2}(0,2a+1,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|=\bigg|(I)+\frac{(1+2a)\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a^{2}+4a+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(-(1+2a)K_{1}\sigma)}{2\Phi(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n},

(F.57)

and

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\frac{A_{5}(0,a+1,a,K_{1})A_{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|=\bigg|(II)+\frac{a\sigma^{2}K_{2}e^{\frac{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}}{2}}\Phi(-(1+a)K_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi(-aK_{1}\sigma)}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.58)

Specifically, by considering Taylor’s expansion at
K_{1}=0
of the function above, we can obtain

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\big[K_{2}(II)\big]+a\sigma^{2}e^{(2a+1)\sigma^{2}/2}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{d}

(F.59)

Substituting the results of (
F.57
), (
F.58
), and (
F.59
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.13
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-
dimensional sphere distribution, and we denote
K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\big)^{2}}\bigg]+a\sigma^{4}(1+a^{2}\sigma^{2})\Bigg|\leq\frac{f_{23}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{f_{24}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

Here,
f_{23}(a,\sigma)
,
f_{24}(a,\sigma)
are both analytic functions of
a
and
\sigma
while irrelevant with
n
and
d
.

Proof of Lemma
F.13
.

Following a similar technique utilized in the proof of previous lemmas, that
\frac{1}{S^{2}}=\int_{0}^{\infty}se^{-sS}ds
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}^{2}}{\big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\big)^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}^{2}\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-2aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-2aK_{2}x_{1}}x_{1}^{3}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-aK_{2}(x_{1}+x_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-2aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|-2aK_{2}x_{1}}x_{1}^{3}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-aK_{2}(x_{1}+x_{2})}x_{1}x_{2}^{2}e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{6}(s/n,2a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-1}\mathrm{d}s}_{(I)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}sA_{2}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})A_{1}(s/n,a,a,K_{1})[M(s/n,a,K_{1})]^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s}_{(II)}\bigg].

(F.60)

Here,
A_{1}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
,
A_{2}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
and
M(\lambda,a,K_{1})
share the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.1
, and
A_{6}(\lambda,\alpha,a,K_{1})
shares the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.7
. By utilizing the Lipschitz continuity of these terms established previously, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{A_{6}(0,2a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|=\bigg|(I)+\frac{a\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}(3+4a^{2}\sigma^{2}K_{2}^{2})\Phi(-2aK_{1}\sigma)}{\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n},

(F.61)

and

\displaystyle\bigg|(II)+\frac{A_{2}(0,a,a,K_{1})A_{1}(0,a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|=\bigg|(II)+\sigma^{4}aK_{2}(1+a^{2}K_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2})\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}.

(F.62)

Specifically, by considering Taylor’s expansion of the function above at
K_{1}=0
, we can further derive that

\displaystyle\big|\mathbb{E}[K_{2}(II)]+a\sigma^{4}(1+a^{2}\sigma^{2})\big|\leq\frac{c_{2}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{c_{3}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

(F.63)

Substituting the results of (
F.61
), (
F.62
), and (
F.63
) into (
F.1
), we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma F.14
.

Let

x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables, and
\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
be another
n
i.i.d Gaussian random variables. In addition,
a
is a positive scalar, and
\bm{\theta}_{*},\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are two independent random vectors following uniform
d-

K_{1}=\langle\bm{\theta}_{0},\bm{\theta}_{*}\rangle
,
K_{2}=\|(\mathbf{I}_{d}-\bm{\theta}_{*}\bm{\theta}_{*}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|}{\big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\big)^{2}}\bigg]+\frac{2a\sigma^{4}}{\pi}\Bigg|\leq\frac{f_{25}(a,\sigma)}{n}+\frac{f_{26}(a,\sigma)}{d}.

and

and

.

Proof of Lemma
F.14
.

F.13
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{K_{2}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{2}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|}{\big(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\big)^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}|+|\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|)-aK_{2}(x_{i_{1}}+x_{i_{2}})}x_{i_{1}}|\widetilde{x}_{i_{1}}||\widetilde{x}_{i_{2}}|\exp\!\Big(\!-\!s^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{-aK_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{i}|-aK_{2}x_{i}}\Big)\bigg]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-aK_{2}(x_{1}+x_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|e^{-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-s^{\prime}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{n-1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[K_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}s\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-aK_{1}(|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+|\widetilde{x}_{2}|)-aK_{2}(x_{1}+x_{2})}x_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}||\widetilde{x}_{2}|e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{2}|+K_{2}x_{2})}}|K_{1}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[e^{-\frac{s}{n}e^{-a(K_{1}|\widetilde{x}_{1}|+K_{2}x_{1})}}|K_{1}\big]\Big)^{n-2}\mathrm{d}s\bigg]

and

shares the same definitions as in the proof in Lemma
F.8
.
Through a similar technique to utilize the Lipschitz continuity of these terms, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg|(I)+\frac{A_{8}(0,2a,a,K_{1})}{4e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg|(I)+\frac{a\sigma^{4}K_{2}e^{a^{2}\sigma^{2}}\big((1+4a^{2}K_{1}^{2}\sigma^{2})\Phi(-2aK_{1}\sigma)-2aK_{1}\sigma\phi(2aK_{1}\sigma)\big)}{\Phi^{2}(-a\sigma K_{1})}\Bigg|\leq\frac{c_{1}(a,\sigma)}{n},

and

(F.67)

Substituting the results of (
F.1
), (
F.1
), and (

) into (

), we complete the proof.
